Upstream proxies vary from coarse layouts to fine articulated geometry depending on the interaction, and streaming inference must operate in a few causal steps without accumulating drift across self-generated history. We present Invoker-Render, pairing a bidirectional creation model with a causal four-step streaming model under a shared conditioning interface over structural proxies, an appearance reference, and language. Trained on an aligned approximately 200-hour multi-granularity corpus, Invoker-Render honors fine geometric constraints when supplied while synthesizing plausible local motion under coarse proxies, enabling runtime selection of input detail. To bridge bidirectional creation and real-time streaming without compounding step compression and rollout drift, we introduce Progressive Forcing (ProForcing). ProForcing first trains a causal predictor and distills it into a four-step renderer using histories derived from training videos. Training then shifts to continuous self-generated rollouts. A pipelined serving stack supports efficient streaming inference. Experiments show versatile control across proxy families, granularities, and camera paths, consistent gains over recent streaming baselines, and stable 30-minute racing rollouts that preserve scene and subject appearance.

</details>


### [136] [Fiberpose: state-of-the-art fiber morphometry and instance segmentation with a distance-transform neural network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22953)
*Viktor A. Balashov,Daniil S. Antonenko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Fiberpose 框架，将 SEM 图像中的纤维分析重构为平滑距离变换(DT)场的直接回归，并配套几何算法在同一流程内完成实例重建、直径与取向测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纤维定量方法多依赖经典二值化加简单后处理，在弱边界、对比度不均和纤维密集的图像上结果不可靠，缺乏统一、可复现的定量流程。

Method: 以平滑距离变换场作为回归目标，由网络直接预测该场以编码纤维几何；并设计一组适配该表示的新型几何算法，用于实例重建及直径、取向测量。实现为独立 Python 桌面应用与网页工具 fiberpose.org。

Result: 在独立纤维 SEM 数据集上，实例分割精度优于 SAM2 与 Cellpose-SAM，直径测量精度优于 DiameterJ、Fibar 与 GIFT；在密集与低对比区域仍有效，并对训练数据未覆盖的显微模态表现出定性泛化。

Conclusion: Fiberpose 提供了可解释的几何表示与自动化流水线，可实现纤维材料可复现的定量表征。

Abstract: Analysis of fibrous structures in SEM images is a routine task in biomedical engineering and materials science. However, most available methods for fiber quantification still rely on classical image binarization followed by simple post-processing. These approaches often produce unreliable results in images with weak boundaries, heterogeneous contrast, and densely packed fibers. Here, we introduce Fiberpose, a framework that reformulates fiber analysis as direct regression of a smooth distance-transform (DT) field, together with a novel set of geometry-based algorithms specifically adapted to this representation. The predicted field directly encodes fiber geometry, while the accompanying algorithms enable instance reconstruction and measurement of diameter and orientation within a unified workflow. The framework is implemented as a standalone Python desktop application and a web tool https://fiberpose.org. On an independent dataset of fibrous SEM images, Fiberpose achieved state-of-the-art instance-segmentation accuracy in comparison with SAM2 and Cellpose-SAM, and more accurate diameter measurements than DiameterJ, Fibar and GIFT. The method remains effective in dense and weakly contrasted regions and shows qualitative generalization across microscopy modalities not represented in the training data. Fiberpose therefore provides an interpretable geometric representation and an automated pipeline for reproducible quantitative characterization of fibrous materials.

</details>


### [137] [Is model self-assessment enough for diverse LVLM ensembles?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22956)
*Michael Smith,Frank P. Ferrie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建统一的无训练集成流水线（候选回答选择），系统评测34种置信度估计、24种集成配置、20个数据集与2种提示词，发现仅依赖各模型自评置信度的简单无参数方法即可超越单个成员模型，但数据集、集成组成与提示词措辞是显著影响集成效果的关键混淆变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）集成在多个数据集和任务上优于单一模型，但现有聚合过程通常依赖训练、参数拟合或复杂的任务专用流水线，且评估不充分，难以进行公平比较。作者希望建立一个统一、可公平比较的评测框架，以检验简单方法是否已足够。

Method: 提出统一流水线，将集成建模为候选回答选择（candidate response selection）：由不同模型提供候选答案，再从中选出最佳回答。在此框架下全面评测34种置信度估计方法、24种集成配置、20个数据集和2种提示词规格，比较无训练、无参数的置信度自评方法与需训练/校准的更复杂方案。

Result: 简单的无训练、无参数自评方法（仅依赖各成员模型自身置信度、无需显式校准）即可优于单个成员模型，且在准确率等标准指标上（含开放式问题）与更复杂方法具竞争力，并可跨不同架构集成。但同时发现数据集、集成组成和提示词措辞是此前研究探索不足的关键混淆变量，会显著影响集成表现，甚至使集成劣于最优成员模型；为取得最佳效果需针对这些因素进行优化，代价是牺牲泛化性。

Conclusion: 若部署条件能被精确刻画，可采用无训练集成；建议以强模型的 Best-of-N 配置作为起点。集成性能高度依赖数据集、组成与提示词， practitioners 不应假定集成总能优于其最佳成员模型。

Abstract: Ensembles of diverse Large Vision Language Models have demonstrated performance beyond that of single models across multiple datasets and tasks. However, the necessary aggregation process usually relies on training, parameter fitting, or complex task-specific pipelines. Furthermore, evaluation is limited, making a fair comparison difficult. We create a unified pipeline where we perform candidate response selection: we build an ensemble by selecting the best answer from a set provided by different models. We comprehensively evaluate across 34 different means of confidence estimation, 24 ensemble configurations, 20 datasets, and two prompt specifications. We show that simple training and parameter-free approaches can be sufficient, even for ensembles of different architectures. These simple self-assessment approaches rely solely on each constituent model's confidence, without explicit calibration, yet are able to perform better than said constituent models. They are also competitive with more involved alternatives on standard metrics such as accuracy, including on open-ended problems. However, we also discover that dataset, ensemble composition, and prompt wording are key confounding variables insufficiently explored by prior work that significantly affect ensemble performance, such that the ensemble can perform worse than its best constituent model. These must be optimized for to get the most out of ensembles, at the cost of limiting their generalizability. We recommend that practitioners consider training-free ensembles only if deployment conditions can be precisely characterized, with Best-of-N configurations of strong models being a good starting point.

</details>


### [138] [Hierarchical Diffusion Transformer for End-to-End Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22962)
*Xiaoyu Wu,Yifei Wang,Weihang Guo,Chen Wei,Lydia E Kavraki,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Steven Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiT（Hierarchical Diffusion Transformers）在像素空间扩散的骨干网络中引入可学习的层级令牌分组机制，将相关patch令牌聚合为组令牌并在瓶颈处处理，无需外部视觉预训练即可显著提升生成质量与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散可端到端联合学习视觉表征与图像生成，免去预训练视觉编码器，但普通像素空间Transformer在生成质量与训练效率上仍落后于两阶段潜空间扩散模型。作者假设部分差距源于骨干网络全程保持"一patch一token"，未显式鼓励逐级粗化的视觉层级结构。

Method: 提出HiT：逐步将相关令牌分组，每组用单个令牌表示；主Transformer瓶颈仅处理紧凑的组令牌，再通过ungroup还原至原图像位置特征。引入压缩预算（compression budget）约束批内平均压缩比，使分组归属与组数可随图像和扩散时间步动态变化（训练与生成阶段均如此）。

Result: ImageNet 256×256上达1.48 FID；生成质量与JiT相当而累积训练FLOPs约为其40%。文生图中，在五个基准上的平均分超过UniLiP，去噪器参数量约少6倍、前向FLOPs约少3倍。模型涌现语义结构：无分割标签即可将连贯物体及物体部件分组；无区域标注即可将caption词与描绘相同概念的图像patch分组。

Conclusion: 在生成器内部学习视觉层级可打破"无外部视觉预训练即难以实现强图像生成"的壁垒，说明层级化令牌组织是像素空间扩散追平乃至超越潜空间扩散的有效途径。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion learns visual representations and image generation jointly in a single end-to-end training stage, without a separately pretrained visual encoder. However, plain pixel-space Transformers still trail leading two-stage latent diffusion models in generation quality and training efficiency. We hypothesize that part of this gap arises from retaining one token per patch throughout the backbone, which does not explicitly encourage a visual hierarchy that summarizes related content at progressively coarser levels. To address this, we introduce Hierarchical Diffusion Transformers (HiT), which progressively group related tokens and represent each group with a single token. The main Transformer bottleneck processes these compact group tokens, and ungrouping restores features to their original image positions. A compression budget regulates the average compression ratio across the batch, allowing group memberships and counts to vary across images and diffusion timesteps during both training and generation. The model exhibits emergent semantic structure, learning to group coherent objects and object parts without segmentation labels. In text-to-image generation, HiT further learns to group caption words with image patches depicting the same concepts, without region annotations. HiT achieves 1.48 FID on ImageNet $256\times256$ and reaches comparable generation quality to JiT using approximately 40\% of its cumulative training FLOPs. For text-to-image generation, HiT exceeds UniLiP's average score across five benchmarks with roughly $6\times$ fewer denoiser parameters and $3\times$ fewer forward FLOPs. These results suggest that learning a visual hierarchy within the generator breaks down the barrier to stronger image generation without external visual pretraining.

</details>


### [139] [Training-Free Building Change Detection with Example-Induced Discriminants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22964)
*Zhi Lu,Bin Hong,Zhenming Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LIND是一个免训练框架，利用少量变更标注将区域特征对比转化为可复用的线性建筑分类器，在单一冻结ViT特征空间中统一建筑识别与时相比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑变化检测依赖密集标注，跨区域采集成本高；少量标注示例虽经济，但变化图仅标注变化建筑、未指明含建筑日期，属于不完整监督。

Method: 关键观察：这些标注仍蕴含可识别其他图像（含未变化图像）中建筑的特征。LIND将区域特征对比转为线性判别分类器，仅用单一冻结ViT，不需文本提示或辅助分割模型；仅在一个验证集上调一个参数，并跨数据集与样本设置固定。

Result: 大量实验显示检测精度高、跨数据集迁移能力强、推理高效，并可扩展到其他语义类别与更广的土地覆盖变化。

Conclusion: 变更标注可为新场景检测提供紧凑的视觉规格说明，实现低成本、可迁移的建筑变化检测。

Abstract: Building change detection (BCD) relies on dense annotations that are costly to collect across regions. A few annotated examples offer a more economical way to specify the target, but change maps provide incomplete building supervision: they label only changed buildings and leave the building-containing date unspecified. Our key observation is that these annotations nevertheless reveal features that recognize buildings in other images, including unchanged ones. We present Linear INduced Discriminants (LIND), a training-free framework that turns regional feature contrasts into a reusable linear building classifier. Using a single frozen vision transformer, it unifies building recognition and temporal comparison in the same feature space. This approach requires neither text prompts nor an auxiliary segmentation model. Only one parameter is tuned on a single validation dataset and then kept fixed across datasets and shot settings. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong detection accuracy, cross-dataset transfer, and efficient inference, with extensions to other semantic classes and broader land-cover changes. Change annotations thus provide a compact visual specification for detection in new scenes. Code: https://github.com/lzcn/lind

</details>


### [140] [MI-Hyper: Generating Multiple Instance Models for Weight Space Transfer Learning in Histopathology via Mixed Tasks Pretrained HyperNetwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22968)
*Xitong Ling,Yousef Kotp,Weiming Chen,Yining Li,Jiawen Li,Minxi Ouyang,Xinyi Guo,Mingxi Fu,Yizhi Wang,Tian Guan,Lianghui Zhu,Yonghong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MI-Hyper 通过超网络按任务描述生成 MIL 聚合与分类权重，实现无需参数更新的全切片图像零样本预测，在 308 个任务上优于 MI-Zero。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本 WSI 预测需在部署时不更新参数即泛化到新病理任务；现有 MI-Zero 依赖 patch-text 相似度池化，本文探索从任务描述学习构造预测规则（条件权重生成）这一替代路径。

Method: 提出 MI-Hyper：在混合任务上预训练超网络，生成 MIL 模型的聚合权重与分类权重。两阶段训练——先用源标签联合训练生成器与共享 MIL 基座，再冻结基座、精化生成器；生成的任务权重可在多个样本间复用。

Result: 在 52 个病理数据集共 308 个任务上，相较 MI-Zero 平均 AUC、平衡准确率、macro-F1 分别提升 1.85、1.17、0.57 个百分点；部分分子预测任务上 AUC 与平衡准确率提升超过 20 点。

Conclusion: 任务条件化的权重生成是面向长序列建模的权重空间迁移的一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Zero-shot whole-slide image (WSI) prediction aims to generalize to new pathology tasks without parameter updates at deployment. MI-Zero makes these predictions by pooling patch-text similarities. Conditional weight generation, combined with supervision from diverse pathology tasks, offers an alternative: learning to construct predictive rules from task descriptions. We introduce MI-Hyper, a hypernetwork pretrained across mixed tasks to generate the aggregation and classification weights of multiple instance learning (MIL) models. We first train the generator and a shared MIL basis jointly using source labels. We then freeze the basis and refine the generator. The generated task weights are reused across cases. Across 308 tasks from 52 pathology datasets, MI-Hyper improves mean AUC, balanced accuracy and macro-F1 over MI-Zero by 1.85, 1.17 and 0.57 percentage points, respectively. On some molecular prediction tasks, both AUC and balanced accuracy improve by more than 20 points. These results support task-conditioned weight generation as a promising approach to weight-space transfer for long-sequence modeling.

</details>


### [141] [Rescaling OLS-Merged Adapters To Improve Personalized Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22969)
*Leander Girrbach,Niklas Schweiger,Zeynep Akata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出基于OLS的多适配器合并目标在任务激活分布重叠时会系统性削弱任务特定信号，并提出对合并更新进行重缩放以恢复信号强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将多个适配器合并进单一图像生成模型可降低部署成本并保留多样化任务能力或个性化模式。但现有SOTA方法（RegMean、Mix-of-Show、Subspace Routing）用普通最小二乘（OLS）拟合个体适配器输出，导致合并适配器在下游使用时过弱。

Method: 推导出量化该信号收缩的分解形式，并据此对合并后的更新量进行重缩放（rescaling），以恢复任务特定信号的强度。

Result: 在最多合并30个适配器的多主体个性化任务上，重缩放相比未缩放基线显著提升主体保真度；在风格迁移、概念擦除、图像分类及基于LLM的文本分类任务上同样取得改进。

Conclusion: OLS式重构目标存在固有缺陷，简单重缩放即可有效缓解信号收缩，且方法具跨任务与跨模态的通用性。

Abstract: Merging multiple adapters into a single image generation model reduces deployment costs while preserving diverse task capabilities or personalization modes. State-of-the-art methods such as RegMean, Mix-of-Show, and Subspace Routing fit the outputs of the merged model to those of the individual adapters using ordinary least squares (OLS). We show that this reconstruction objective systematically shrinks task-specific signals whenever the activation distributions of different tasks overlap, producing a merged adapter that is too weak for downstream use. We derive a decomposition that quantifies this shrinkage and propose rescaling the merged update to restore signal strength. On multi-subject personalization with up to 30 merged adapters, rescaling substantially improves subject fidelity over unscaled baselines. Our approach also improves results on style transfer, concept erasure, image classification, and LLM-based text classification.

</details>


### [142] [SpikWM: Efficient End-to-End Spiking World Modeling for Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22981)
*Shuhan Ye,Hongbin Yu,Chong Wang,Hui Lu,Jiaming He,Yi Yu,Jiangbo Qian,Xudong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpikWM：面向目标条件规划的脉冲潜世界模型，基于JEPA架构，每张图像单步仿真、每个潜位置一次神经更新，以脉冲编码保留空间与运动线索，在多个视觉控制任务上超越脉冲基线并显著降低算术能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像观测与目标条件下的规划需要有能区分视觉状态与动作后果的预测模型。脉冲神经网络计算稀疏，但单步脉冲易丢失运动线索，且会把物理后果不同的动作混为一谈，因此需要设计能保留空间信息与动作区分度的脉冲世界模型。

Method: 提出Spiking Latent World Model（SpikWM），端到端联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），每张图像一个仿真步、每个潜位置一次神经更新。含三个模块：Spiking Regional Encoding通过区域池化保留空间线索；Bi-Spike Action Encoding用正负二值响应组合编码动作；Causal Membrane Prediction借助可学习的分组时间常数将亚阈值证据沿潜历史传递。编码器与预测器在无奖励的图像–动作轨迹上以next-embedding预测与SIGReg联合训练。

Result: 在统一规划器下，Push-T、Two-Room、Reacher、OGBench-Cube平均成功率分别为88.67%、97.33%、99.33%、86.67%，优于两个脉冲基线；Push-T消融支持各模块有效性，受控回放检验了对外观与物理状态变化的响应；在同等规划预算下，相对LeWM降低约75.6%的估计算术能耗。

Conclusion: SpikWM表明脉冲JEPA世界模型可在视觉目标条件规划中兼顾性能与能效，为稀疏计算的世界模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned planning with image observations and goals requires predictions that distinguish visual states and action consequences. Spiking neural networks offer sparse computation, but single-step spikes can obscure motion cues and merge actions with different physical outcomes. We propose the Spiking Latent World Model (SpikWM), an end-to-end joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) with one simulation step per image and one neural update per latent position. Spiking Regional Encoding retains spatial cues through regional pooling. Bi-Spike Action Encoding combines positive and negative binary responses, while Causal Membrane Prediction carries subthreshold evidence across the latent history with learned group-wise time constants. The encoder and predictor are trained jointly on reward-free image--action trajectories using next-embedding prediction and SIGReg. With a common planner, SpikWM achieves mean success rates of $88.67\%$, $97.33\%$, $99.33\%$, and $86.67\%$ on Push-T, Two-Room, Reacher, and OGBench-Cube, respectively, outperforming two spiking baselines. Push-T ablations support each module, and controlled replays examine responses to appearance and physical state changes. Under a matched planning budget, SpikWM reduces estimated arithmetic energy by $75.6\%$ relative to LeWM. Code will be released at https://github.com/ShawnYE618/SpikWM.

</details>


### [143] [CAST: Calibrating Correspondences for Training-Free Biomedical In-Context Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22995)
*Mingze Jiang,Jiashu Han,Haonan Chen,Zhiyi Li,Yilun Du,Kunzan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架CAST，基于冻结DINOv3骨干，通过"稳定性测试"与"连贯性测试"校准生物医学图像细粒度对应关系，实现上下文分割泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学图像分割需快速适应新解剖结构、受试者、模态与采集协议，但稠密专家标注稀缺，跨队列迁移困难。上下文分割（ICS）以少量推理时标注样例指定新结构，但生物医学图像中相邻结构外观相似、跨受试者与成像条件变化大，可靠对应仍具挑战。

Method: CAST为免训练框架，构建于冻结的DINOv3骨干之上。包含两项测试：稳定性测试在全局与patch特定层面提升特征可靠性；连贯性测试评估候选对应是否由一致的局部结构上下文支持。二者共同筛选出更可靠的对应关系。

Result: 在覆盖2D与3D分割、跨受试者及跨模态迁移的八个生物医学基准上，CAST较最强整体基线Dice提升12.34个百分点，并在全部六个体数据（3D）设置中排名第一；进一步支持2D→3D分割与图谱标签迁移等挑战性应用。

Conclusion: 通过稳定性与连贯性双重校准的免训练对应框架，可显著提升生物医学上下文分割的跨结构、跨条件泛化能力，并拓展至体数据与图谱迁移场景。

Abstract: Biomedical image segmentation must keep up with new anatomical structures, subjects, imaging modalities, and acquisition protocols, yet dense expert annotations are scarce and models trained on one cohort often struggle to transfer. In-context segmentation (ICS) offers a way out: a new structure is specified by a few annotated exemplars at inference time. However, reliable correspondence remains challenging in biomedical images, where adjacent structures often share similar appearances, and variations across subjects and imaging conditions further obscure their distinctions. We introduce CAST, a training-free framework built on a frozen DINOv3 backbone to calibrate fine-grained correspondences. Its stability test improves feature reliability at both global and patch-specific levels, while its coherence test assesses whether candidate correspondences are supported by consistent local structural context. Together, these tests enable more reliable correspondence across diverse anatomical structures and imaging conditions. Across eight biomedical benchmarks spanning 2D and 3D segmentation, cross-subject, and cross-modality transfer, CAST outperforms the strongest overall baseline by 12.34 percentage points in Dice and ranks first across all six volumetric settings. The versatility of CAST further opens up opportunities for challenging biomedical applications, including 2D-to-3D segmentation and atlas label transfer.

</details>


### [144] [The Rhythm of Generated Video: Latent Chunking Leaves Periodic Traces in Compressed Bitstreams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.22996)
*Chen Ye Gan,Bryan Zhen Zhang,Gene Wen,Yuxing han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需解码像素的AI生成视频检测方法：利用因果视频自编码器在压缩码流中留下的周期性"比特脉冲"（架构指纹）来区分生成视频与真实相机视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频检测器大多分析解码后的像素，忽略了视频的生成与编码过程本身，因而错失了生成架构在码流层面留下的可辨识痕迹。

Method: 揭示因果视频自编码器的时间步幅会将不可预测内容集中在隐变量块（latent-chunk）边界，使率失真优化编码器周期性分配比特；据此构建免训练的峰值比（peak-ratio）统计量，直接在压缩码流的帧大小轨迹上检测周期性脉冲，并进一步用于生成器来源识别。

Result: 在CogVideoX与Wan2.2中观测到4帧周期脉冲，LTX-Video为8帧，内容匹配的相机视频无此现象；仅将相机视频过一遍生成器自编码器即可诱发该脉冲，支持其成因假设。检测AUC达0.84–0.94，且在多种量化设置及H.264/H.265编码下保持稳健；帧大小轨迹可识别4个商用生成器，H.264/H.265/AV1下准确率分别为89.2%/84.1%/83.0%，优于基于像素的UnivFD基线（72.7–73.6%）。

Conclusion: 研究将生成架构与可测量的编码需求直接关联，确立了码流层"架构节奏"作为生成视频检测与溯源的新线索，并为免解码、低成本的视频取证提供了依据。

Abstract: Most AI-generated video detectors analyze decoded pixels and overlook how videos are produced. We show that causal video autoencoders leave an architecture-specific rhythm in compressed bitstreams: their temporal stride concentrates unpredictable content at latent-chunk boundaries, so rate-distortion optimized encoders allocate bits periodically. Without decoding pixels, we observe four-frame pulses in CogVideoX and Wan2.2 and eight-frame pulses in LTX-Video, which are absent from content-matched camera footage. Passing camera footage through a generator's autoencoder alone induces the pulse, supporting its proposed origin. A training-free peak-ratio statistic separates generated from camera video (AUC 0.84-0.94) and persists across tested quantization settings and H.264/H.265 encoding. Frame-size traces also identify four commercial generators, with 89.2%, 84.1%, and 83.0% accuracy under H.264, H.265, and AV1, beating the pixel-based UnivFD baseline (72.7-73.6%). Our results directly link generative architecture to measurable coding demand.

</details>


### [145] [Tracking Ethological Behaviors of Broiler Chickens Through Computer Vision (YoloV11) Using an Android Mobile Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23007)
*Ryan Davi Oliveira de Meneses,Glauber da Rocha Balthazar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于YOLOv11与TFLite的肉鸡行为（坐、站、采食）自动分类模型，部署于Android端并验证其在真实禽舍中的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为肉鸡行为提供连续、自动化的监测手段，支撑早期福利评估与精准养殖决策，减少人工观察的主观性与成本，并为商用规模移动端应用奠定技术基础。

Method: 使用公开视频，经预处理与人工标注为三类（sitting、standing、feeding）；先做探索性分析以保证数据集一致性与图像质量；训练YOLOv11模型，经TFLite转换部署到Android应用；在未见图像与真实农场环境中评估。

Result: 分类性能呈类别依赖：静态姿态较稳健（sitting AP=0.531、precision=100%），动态行为较弱（feeding AP=0.292、recall=0.20）；未见图像测试准确率显著提升，sitting 98.04%、standing 83.33%、feeding 67.65%；真实禽舍中表现出有效泛化能力。

Conclusion: 模型可支撑持续行为监测、早期福利评估与精准养殖决策，为商用规模化移动应用奠定技术基础，凸显计算机视觉在自动化动物福利评估中的作用；动态采食行为识别仍是主要改进方向。

Abstract: This study developed and evaluated a computer vision model for automated ethological behavior classification of broiler chickens in climate-controlled poultry houses. Using publicly available videos preprocessed and manually annotated into three classes (sitting, standing, and feeding), the YOLOv11 model was trained and deployed on an Android application via TFLite conversion. Exploratory analysis ensured dataset consistency and visual quality, while evaluation showed class-dependent performance: higher robustness for static postures (sitting, AP=0.531; precision=100%) and lower accuracy for dynamic behaviors (feeding, AP=0.292; recall=0.20). Tests with unseen images demonstrated significant improvements, achieving accuracies of 98.04% for sitting, 83.33% for standing, and 67.65% for feeding. The model showed effective generalization in real farm environments, supporting its potential for continuous behavioral monitoring, early welfare assessments, and decision-making in precision poultry farming. These findings establish a technical foundation for future mobile-based applications at commercial scale, reinforcing the role of computer vision in automated animal welfare evaluation.

</details>


### [146] [When a Larger Guidance Scale Repairs Text-to-Video Failures and When It Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23025)
*Taewon Kang,Matthias Zwicker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文是一篇测量研究，考察"提高classifier-free guidance scale"这一常用修复手段何时有效，并提出基于prompt特征与"demand"分数的先验判定条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当text-to-video扩散模型忽略部分prompt时，业界通常第一反应是提高CFG scale，但这种方法是否普遍有效缺乏系统测量，用户也缺少事先判断其是否值得尝试的依据。

Method: 在Mochi模型上针对三类失败（罕见组合、否定、晚期事件）测量提高scale与重新生成的修复效果对比；提出"demand"分数，衡量denoiser对被遗漏prompt部分的反应强度，从去噪前四分之一阶段、视频尚未生成时读取；以已发表的针对三类失败的修正方法作为固定探针进行验证。

Result: 提高scale对罕见组合（"a snowy beach"）的修复优于重新生成；对否定（"a highway with no vehicles"）无效，修复失败数多于重新生成；对须在片段后期发生的事件（沙堡被浪冲平）无任何改变。主要发现为一个条件：仅当prompt不含否定、不含绑定特定时间的事件，且demand足够高时，提高scale才有效，且阈值以上恢复率明显更高。

Conclusion: 提出可事先判断的决策规则：用户在重新生成前即可判断最常见的修复手段是否值得一试，以及不适用时应改用何种方法。

Abstract: When a text-to-video diffusion model ignores part of a prompt, the usual first fix is to raise the classifier-free guidance scale. This paper is a measurement study of when that fix works. On Mochi, the same increase repairs failures on rare compositions ("a snowy beach") beyond what generating again does, does not repair negations ("a highway with no vehicles"), where it fixes fewer failures than generating again does, and changes nothing for events that must happen late in a clip ("a sandcastle that a wave washes flat"). Which of these happens can be known in advance, from the prompt and one inexpensive score. The score, which we call demand, measures how strongly the denoiser reacts to the part of the prompt that the failed video leaves out, and it is read from the first quarter of denoising, before any video exists. Our main finding is a condition: raising the scale is effective only when the prompt contains no negation and no event tied to a specific time, and when demand is sufficiently high, with recovery clearly higher above a fitted threshold than below it. Published corrections for the three failure types, used as fixed probes, behave as this account predicts. A short decision rule follows: before generating again, a user can tell whether the most common fix is worth trying, and what to use when it is not.

</details>


### [147] [Check Your Blind Spots: Decision-Guided Perception by Probing the Planner with Hypothetical Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23030)
*Hoang Pham Cong,Long D. Nguyen,Ho Viet Duc Luong,Nguyen Tran Minh Nhat,Binh P. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出决策引导的推测式感知（DGSP），用规划器的决策敏感性来确定高分辨率计算的位置，以约1/4像素在约98%帧上复现全帧高清感知的规划动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并非所有感知误差都会改变规划器动作，而现有自适应分辨率感知依赖图像线索选择高分辨率区域，既无法可靠指向低分辨率漏检的目标，也无法判断哪些目标对规划器重要。

Method: 将低分辨率场景及其动作视为草稿，在候选位置插入假设目标并探测规划器是否改变动作，得到"决策敏感性场"；仅对敏感性场与当前动作所依赖的"支撑集"（检测结果）进行高分辨率验证，迭代直至动作稳定。

Result: 对两类规划器证明：当裁剪检测与全帧检测一致且裁剪覆盖敏感性场时，DGSP 返回与全帧高分辨率感知完全相同的动作（真实图像近似满足）。三个驾驶数据集上，约1/4像素成本下约98%帧复现该动作；与均匀降采样同等像素成本下，修复更多草稿分歧、误刹车更少、找回更多决策相关漏检目标。

Conclusion: 以规划器决策为中心分配感知算力，可在显著降低像素开销的同时保持决策等价性，优于图像线索驱动的自适应分辨率方案。

Abstract: Not every perception error changes the planner's action. Yet adaptive-resolution perception chooses where to spend high-resolution computation from image cues, which can neither reliably point to an object that low resolution missed nor tell which objects matter to the planner. We observe that the planner can say where an object would matter before anything is seen: inserting a hypothetical object at a location tests whether its action would change. Decision-Guided Speculative Perception (DGSP) treats the low-resolution scene and its action as a draft and probes the planner in this way to obtain a decision-sensitivity field, the locations where an unseen object would change the action. Only this field and the support, the detections on which the current action rests, are verified at high resolution, repeatedly until the action is stable. For two planner classes, we prove that DGSP returns exactly the action of whole-frame high-resolution perception if crop detections match whole-frame ones and the crops cover the field, conditions that real images meet approximately. On three driving datasets, it reproduces this action on about 98% of frames with about a quarter of the pixels. At the pixel cost of uniform downscaling, it repairs more of the draft's disagreements with this action, brakes falsely less often, and recovers many more of the decision-relevant objects that the draft misses.

</details>


### [148] [M3OPD-Reasoner: An Open Recipe for Post-Training Multimodal Reasoning with Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23034)
*Nguyen Quang Trung,Hoang Phan,Shuo Sun,Longyin Zhang,AiTi Aw*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出M3OPD-Reasoner，一套面向视觉语言模型的三领域（图像STEM、图像感知、视频）后训练全开源配方。核心结论：先用GSPO直接为各领域训练教师并做多教师on-policy蒸馏（MOPD）优于混合数据训练；再在蒸馏学生上叠加MixRL，可让同一批数据继续提升模型且不崩溃，形成"MOPD→MixRL"两阶段范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM后训练缺乏跨域（图像STEM/图像感知/视频）系统性比较：混合数据训练（MixSFT/MixRL）与多教师蒸馏孰优不明；且缺少一个完全开放（数据、代码、各阶段checkpoint）的多域推理后训练配方，也缺乏"蒸馏后再RL能否在同一数据上继续增益"的实证研究。

Method: 以Qwen3.5-9B为起点，构建M3OPD-SFT-1.35M（SFT）与M3OPD-RL-193K（可验证奖励RL）两套数据，并与21个评测基准做去污染。对比两条路线：(1) 全领域混合数据训练MixSFT与MixRL；(2) 每领域单独训练教师（GSPO，直接或经SFT warm-up），再以多教师on-policy蒸馏（MOPD）合并为单一学生。随后在MOPD学生上以相同prompt与设置再叠加MixRL，检验同数据能否继续增益。

Result: 在21个图像STEM、图像感知与视频推理基准上，经GSPO直接训练教师+MOPD为最佳配方：学生在各教师本领域内接近该教师表现，且在相同prompt上平均优于混合训练基线。MOPD学生作为RL起点更强；在其上叠加MixRL（同prompt、同设置）仍可提升且无性能崩溃，得到主模型M3OPD-Reasoner-9B。

Conclusion: MOPD与MixRL可作为连续两阶段，从同一批数据中获取更多收益；多教师on-policy蒸馏在合并领域能力上优于混合数据训练。作者开源全部数据、训练代码及各阶段checkpoint。

Abstract: Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) enables a single student to absorb the skills of several domain teachers by learning from its own generated responses. We present Multimodal Model Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation Reasoner (M3OPD-Reasoner), a fully open recipe for post-training vision-language models (VLMs) over three reasoning domains: image STEM, image perception, and video. We curate two datasets, M3OPD-SFT-1.35M for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and M3OPD-RL-193K for reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards. On these data, we compare post-training recipes that start from Qwen3.5-9B: training one model on the mixed data of all domains (MixSFT and MixRL), and training one teacher per domain with Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), directly or after an SFT warm-up, and distilling the teachers into one student with MOPD. We evaluate on 21 image STEM, image perception, and video reasoning benchmarks after we decontaminate the training data against them. The comparison establishes MOPD of teachers trained with GSPO directly from Qwen3.5-9B as the best recipe: the student closely matches each teacher on its own domain and is on average above the mixed-training baselines on the same prompts. Since MOPD already merges the domain capabilities that MixRL would otherwise have to learn, its student is a stronger starting point for RL, and we ask whether the same data can carry the model further from there: we add MixRL on top of the MOPD student, on the same prompts and with the same settings, which, to our knowledge, no prior VLM recipe has studied. The same prompts still improve the MOPD student, with no collapse, and give our main model, M3OPD-Reasoner-9B. MOPD and MixRL can therefore work as consecutive stages that get more out of the same data. We release all data, the training code, and the checkpoints of every stage.

</details>


### [149] [Automated Annotation-Free Synthetic Data Generation for Underwater Litter Detection: A Comparison of Generative Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23037)
*Amala Mary Vincent,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种全自动零标注流程：类条件采样包围框→转插入掩码→以复制粘贴/SD inpainting/DALL-E条件生成插入类条件物体，框坐标自动作为标注，用于低数据水下目标检测数据扩充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下目标检测受训练数据稀缺、类别不平衡及浊度、光照、传感器差异导致的外观变化限制；现有几何/颜色增强与GAN全合成缺乏语义真实性或不遵守场景物理，且需额外标注，而低数据域中标注才是核心瓶颈。

Method: 统一的全自动零标注流水线：从采样包围框出发，将每个框转换为插入掩码，在掩码内粘贴或生成类条件物体（垃圾），保持原背景上下文，框坐标直接写入标注；在同一流水线内对比三种策略——复制粘贴、Stable Diffusion inpainting、DALL-E图像条件生成。

Result: 在SeaClear水下碎片数据集上评估包围框保真度、视觉真实感及对下游检测器的影响；DALL-E在5个域中的4个提升检测性能，相对仅真实数据基线最高增益达96%。

Conclusion: 基于掩码内条件生成并自动继承框标注的零标注管线可有效扩充低数据水下检测训练集，其中DALL-E生成策略在多域上带来显著检测提升，优于复制粘贴与SD inpainting。

Abstract: Underwater object detection is often limited by scarce and imbalanced training data and by strong appearance variations caused by turbidity, lighting, and differences between sensors. Existing data augmentation strategies, such as geometric or color augmentation or fully synthetic image generation with Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), either lack semantic realism, fail to respect scene physics, or require additional annotations. In low-data domains like underwater environmental monitoring, the need for additional annotations is often the core bottleneck. Collecting new footage is hard, but labeling this dataset is even more time-consuming and costly, which limits how quickly a usable dataset can be constructed. We propose a unified fully automated zero-annotation pipeline that inserts class-conditioned trash objects into real underwater frames while preserving the original background context. Starting from sampled bounding boxes, each box is converted into an insertion mask and the object is pasted or generated within it, while the same box coordinates are automatically written as the corresponding annotation. Because labels are derived directly from the sampled boxes, the pipeline generates fully labeled training data for low-data settings without any manual annotation. Within this single pipeline we compare three strategies: copy-paste, Stable Diffusion inpainting, and DALL-E image-conditioned generation. We evaluate bounding-box faithfulness, visual realism, and the impact of the generated samples on downstream detector performance on the SeaClear underwater debris dataset. Results show that DALL-E improves detection performance in four of five domains, with relative gains of up to 96 percent over the real-only baseline.

</details>


### [150] [When Anchors Shift the Target: Advantage-Gated Adaptation for Diffusion Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23038)
*Pu Cao,Min Zhou,Ying Shu,Tiezheng Ge,Qing Song,Lu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Advantange-Gated Adaptation (AGA)，通过在正向样本上保留标准回归目标、在负向样本上改用移动策略预测处的一阶切向梯度，解耦移动锚点的双重作用，并用冻结参考模型负责长程行为保持。二分锚点族内唯一确定无参门控 ω(a)=max(-a,0)；实验在组合生成、文本渲染和偏好对齐上均改善奖励-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向过程强化学习（如重加噪后回归）中，负优势会产生无界排斥梯度的凹更新；现有方法用移动策略副本的缓存预测作锚点来稳定训练，但该锚点同时扭曲了正反馈应拟合的目标。作者将这一冲突形式化为"锚点耦合"，并指出仿射移动锚点规则无法在消除负曲率的同时保留全部正回归目标。

Method: 先将冻结参考正则项分离，再解耦移动锚点的双重角色：(1) 正样本保持标准目标拟合，其自限更新在观测目标处消失，与移动锚点无关；(2) 负样本采用凹目标在移动策略预测处的一阶切向，保留局部排斥方向且梯度与预测无关。在二次锚点族内，这些要求唯一确定无参门控 ω(a)=max(-a,0)。长程行为保持交由冻结预训练参考模型，并在其实际运行的 CFG 尺度上正则，以隐式奖励视角解释。

Result: 在组合生成、文本渲染与偏好对齐任务上：位移正目标会降低视觉质量并加剧对策略副本调度（policy-copy schedule）的敏感性；AGA 则稳定提升奖励-质量权衡。

Conclusion: 移动锚点不应承担保留/正则化职责，而仅提供局部线性化；通过对正负样本分别处理并经门控 ω(a) 实现解耦，可无需调参地消除负曲率的破坏性耦合，并将长程保持职责显式交给冻结参考。

Abstract: Forward-process reinforcement learning adapts diffusion and flow-matching models by re-noising rewarded generations into regression targets. Because negative advantages induce concave updates with unbounded repulsive gradients, existing methods stabilize training using cached predictions from a moving policy copy, yet these anchors also alter what positive feedback teaches the model to fit. We formalize this conflict as anchor coupling: after separating frozen-reference regularizers, affine moving-anchor rules cannot eliminate negative curvature while preserving every positive regression target. We propose Advantage-Gated Adaptation (AGA) to decouple these roles. Positive samples retain standard target fitting, whose self-limiting update vanishes at the observed target independently of the moving anchor. Negative samples instead use the first-order tangent of the concave objective at the moving-policy prediction, preserving the local repulsion direction with a prediction-independent gradient. Within the quadratic-anchor family, these requirements uniquely determine the parameter-free gate $ω(a)=\max(-a,0)$. Because the moving copy provides local linearization rather than retention, AGA assigns long-horizon behavior preservation to a frozen pretrained reference regularized at its operating classifier-free guidance scale, interpreted through an implicit-reward lens. Across compositional generation, text rendering, and preference alignment, our experiments show that displacing positive targets degrades visual quality and heightens sensitivity to policy-copy schedules, whereas AGA consistently improves the reward--quality trade-off.

</details>


### [151] [Wall-to-wall maps of canopy height and aboveground carbon across the Atlantic Forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23048)
*Matheus Pinheiro Ferreira,Thiago Carmuega Rabaçal,Giulio Brossi Santoro,Giovanna de Andrade Ferreira,Rodrigo Costa Pinto,Danilo R. A. de Almeida,Paulo Guilherme Molin,Catherine Torres de Almeida,Angélica F. Resende,Vinicius Castro Souza,Marcelo Antonio Pinho Ferreira,Cássio Toledo,Eben North Broadbent,Angelica Almeyda Zambrano,Laura Simões,Juliano van Melis,Ricardo Ribeiro Rodrigues,Pedro H. S. Brancalion*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了2024年巴西大西洋森林10 m冠层高度与30 m地上碳密度高精度制图，RMSE分别为3.05 m和2.58 m，优于全球产品一倍以上，并估算现存森林碳储量1944 Tg C。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球冠层高度图被广泛用于森林碳估算，但其区域精度鲜有检验，直接用于碳核算存在风险。

Method: 以Sentinel-2反射率、Sentinel-1与ALOS PALSAR后向散射及ALOS高程为输入，构建深度学习融合网络预测冠层高度；用圣保罗州机载LiDAR获取的7179个训练斑块与1793个测试斑块（58,800 km²）进行训练与测试，并在圣保罗州外两个LiDAR站点外部验证；基于124个样地拟合幂律关系，将冠层高度转换为地上碳密度。

Result: 测试集RMSE为10 m分辨率3.05 m、30 m分辨率2.58 m；同一像素上Meta与WRI全球产品误差为本方法两倍以上；现存森林在28.3 Mha范围内储存1944 Tg C。

Conclusion: 区域化深度学习制图精度显著优于全球产品，全球产品用于碳核算前必须进行本地验证。

Abstract: Global canopy height maps are increasingly used to estimate forest carbon, yet their regional accuracy is rarely tested. We present wall-to-wall maps of canopy height (10~m) and aboveground carbon density (30~m) for the Brazilian Atlantic Forest in 2024. Height was predicted by a deep learning network fusing Sentinel-2 reflectance, Sentinel-1 and ALOS PALSAR backscatter and ALOS elevation, trained on 7{,}179 and tested on 1{,}793 patches (58{,}800~km$^2$) from a statewide airborne LiDAR campaign over São Paulo state, Brazil. On held-out test patches, the root mean square error is 3.05~m at 10~m and 2.58~m at 30~m, and the model was validated at two LiDAR sites outside São Paulo. Over the same pixels, the Meta and World Resources Institute global product has more than twice this error. A power law fitted to 124 inventory plots converted canopy height to above ground carbon density. The remaining forest holds 1{,}944~Tg~C over 28.3~Mha. Global products therefore need local validation before use in carbon accounting.

</details>


### [152] [Refin3R: Geometry-Guided Refinement for Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23051)
*Jongmin Lee,Jaeyeong Kim,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Refin3R 提出双分支架构，利用 SfM 点云几何先验对前馈三维重建的点图进行稠密化与精化，在多个数据集上精度优于现有方法且速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈三维重建模型直接从多视图预测稠密点图，但缺乏显式几何约束，导致跨视图不一致、精度不足；已有方法虽引入相机位姿或深度等几何先验，却仅作辅助输入、逐视图独立处理，或仅在先验覆盖区域做精化。

Method: 提出 Refin3R，用 SfM 点将几何先验在图像网格上传播。采用双分支架构：2D 分支借助几何感知的骨干特征将 SfM 几何扩展到其覆盖范围之外；3D 分支对所有视图的 SfM 点进行推理，捕获图像网格难以表达的多视图结构；两分支双向交换信息，联合精化每一视图，输出稠密准确点图。

Result: 在 ScanNet++、ETH3D、Tanks&amp;Temples 及域外的人体与手术场景中，Refin3R 的点图精度一致优于前馈方法和几何引导方法，且运行速度快于最强的几何引导基线。

Conclusion: 将 SfM 几何作为可传播的结构先验、并通过 2D/3D 双分支双向交互进行联合精化，是提升前馈点图精度与跨视图一致性的有效途径，并具备跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models predict dense point maps from multiple images in a single pass. Without explicit geometric constraints, however, their predictions are often inconsistent across views and lack fine-grained precision. Recent methods incorporate geometric priors such as camera pose or depth, but use them merely as auxiliary inputs, process each view independently, or refine the geometry only where the priors are available. We present Refin3R, which refines feed-forward point maps with Structure-from-Motion (SfM) points, propagating SfM geometry over the image grid. In its dual-branch architecture, a 2D branch extends SfM geometry beyond its coverage using geometry-aware backbone features, while a 3D branch reasons over the SfM points of all views, capturing multi-view structure that the image grid alone cannot represent. The two branches exchange information bidirectionally to jointly refine each view, yielding dense and accurate point maps. Across ScanNet++, ETH3D, Tanks\&Temples, and out-of-domain human and surgical scenes, Refin3R consistently outperforms feed-forward and geometry-guided methods in point map accuracy while running faster than the strongest geometry-guided baseline.

</details>


### [153] [ReTouchGS: Ultralight Render-free Aesthetic Color Enhancement and Transfer for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23054)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Boyu Wei,Chengkun Yue,Xuanhua Yin,Yiyang Li,Haodong Chen,Qiang Qu,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReTouchGS 是首个面向预训练 3DGS 模型的免参考美学色彩增强框架，通过预测场景级 LUT 实现跨视角一致的色彩增强，并支持参考引导的色彩迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 色彩迁移与交互式编辑依赖参考图像和用户指定的色彩调整，而许多应用只需自动、美学导向的增强，同时保持跨视角色彩与色调一致。此前的方案难以满足这一轻量、无参考、视角一致的需求。

Method: 从预训练 3DGS 模型及其相机参数预测场景级颜色查找表（LUT）；参考引导模式下，用同一场景的一张编辑视图及其对应原始渲染拟合场景级 LUT；采用仅 44.7K 参数的共享 blend-aware 色彩细化网络，对解析色彩更新进行细化，实现无需渲染的色彩映射；并构建 ReTouch3D 基准用于跨视角评估。

Result: 在美学色彩增强与参考引导色彩迁移两类任务上均取得高色彩精度，并对未见场景展现强泛化能力；方法保持几何不变，数秒内输出标准 3DGS 资产。

Conclusion: ReTouchGS 以极轻量的网络实现免参考、视角一致的 3DGS 美学色彩增强与参考引导迁移，兼顾精度、效率与泛化性，并提供了配套基准 ReTouch3D。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables photorealistic scene reconstruction. Existing 3DGS color transfer and interactive editing methods are guided by reference images and user-specified color adjustments. However, in many applications, users simply want aesthetically guided color enhancement that automatically makes the scene more visually appealing while maintaining consistent colors and tones across viewpoints. We present ReTouchGS, to our knowledge the first framework for reference-free aesthetic color enhancement of pretrained 3DGS models. It predicts a scene-level color lookup table (LUT) from the model and its associated camera parameters. ReTouchGS also supports reference-guided color transfer by fitting a scene-level LUT using one edited view of the same scene and its corresponding original render. A shared blend-aware color refinement network with only 44.7K parameters refines an analytic color update to realize either color mapping without rendering. It preserves geometry and delivers standard 3DGS assets in seconds. We also design the ReTouch3D benchmark to support the evaluation of aesthetic color enhancement and reference-guided color transfer across viewpoints. Experiments demonstrate high color accuracy and strong generalization to unseen scenes in both aesthetic color enhancement and reference-guided color transfer.

</details>


### [154] [UnrealEvent: Synthetic Dataset Generation and Annotation for Event-Based Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23078)
*Yentel Schoovaerts,Zijie Ning,Patrick Vandewalle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 UnrealEvent：一个基于 Unreal Engine 5.5 的合成数据集生成工具链，将事件相机仿真（V2E 风格事件帧生成 + 独立采样的对比度阈值）与 EasySynth 语义渲染通道结合，自动产出事件表示与 YOLO 格式框标注，并在 eTraM 上评估 YOLOv8 的合成域、零样本真实域与微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备高时间分辨率、低功耗与大动态范围，但事件域目标检测受限于标注训练数据稀缺，人工标注成本高，因此需要无需手工标注的合成数据生成方案。

Method: 在 UE5.5 中构建渲染—事件—标注一体化流水线：以 EasySynth 语义渲染通道生成逐帧语义与实例信息，再由 V2E 风格生成器根据相邻帧逐像素对数亮度差产生事件，对比度阈值按正态分布独立采样以模拟真实传感器制造引起的阈值失配；渲染关卡序列被自动转换为配对的事件表示与 YOLO 格式边界框标注，无需人工标注。

Result: 在自建合成数据集与 eTraM 上的实验显示：YOLOv8 在合成域内性能较强，但存在显著的合成—真实域间差距，行人类别尤为明显；使用少量真实子集微调可提升真实域性能，残余差距表明资产多样性与传感器标定的重要性。

Conclusion: UnrealEvent 提供了可扩展、免标注的事件检测数据生成途径；合成预训练结合少量真实微调是可行路线，但缩小合成—真实差距仍需提升场景资产多样性与传感器参数标定精度。

Abstract: Event-based cameras provide high temporal resolution, low power consumption, and a high dynamic range, but progress in event-based object detection remains constrained by the scarcity of annotated training data. To solve this issue, this paper introduces the tool UnrealEvent, a synthetic dataset-generation pipeline implemented in Unreal Engine 5.5. The pipeline combines a V2E-inspired event-frame generator with EasySynth semantic-rendering passes, allowing rendered level sequences to be converted into paired event representations and YOLO-format bounding-box annotations without manual labeling. Events are generated from per-pixel log-luminance differences between consecutive frames, with contrast thresholds sampled independently from a normal distribution to emulate the fabrication-induced threshold mismatch of real sensors. Using this pipeline, we provide a synthetic dataset and evaluate YOLOv8 detectors under synthetic, zero-shot real, and real fine-tuning training on eTraM. The results show strong in-domain synthetic performance but a substantial synthetic-to-real gap, especially for pedestrians. Fine-tuning on a small real subset improves real-domain performance, while the remaining gap highlights the importance of asset diversity and sensor calibration.

</details>


### [155] [Transfer Once, Ask Locally: Compact Audio-Visual Memory for On-Device Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23085)
*Qitong Wang,Jihui Jin,Divya Kothandaraman,Vishnu Raj,Christopher Rasmussen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、与查询无关的token剪枝框架，在传输前构建紧凑可复用的多模态记忆，以Monte Carlo Shapley估值和嵌入覆盖目标选择时空/音频"players"，单次传输供本地多问题复用，实现高压缩率下的最优或并列最优QA精度与更低时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量设备上的音视频问答需在速度、内存、隐私与通信间权衡。将音视频编码器放在服务器、轻量语言解码器放在用户端可保问题本地化，但传输并处理完整多模态表示代价仍高，且编码器与问题无关，存在冗余传输。

Method: 将视频时空块与对齐音频窗口定义为players，用Monte Carlo Shapley估值估计其重要性；以嵌入覆盖目标结合查询无关效用与跨模态冗余校准，并通过边际覆盖增益奖励单模态多样性；在有限token预算下保留高分players的原始token嵌入，形成一次性传输、跨后续本地问题复用的紧凑memory。

Result: 在Video-MME、Daily-Omni、Video-Holmes上，基于video-SALMONN 2+(3B)与video-SALMONN 2 Pro(4B)评测，各设置下QA精度均为最优或并列最优；Daily-Omni + 3B在80%剪枝下精度较对比方法提升12.86–14.62个百分点，同时设备端LLM prefill与解码时延最低，在相当嵌入内存下解码加速最高达1.92倍。

Conclusion: 免训练的查询无关token剪枝可在传输前显著压缩多模态记忆，兼顾高压缩率下的精度、设备端时延与内存，适合服务器编码+端侧解码的隐私友好音视频问答部署。

Abstract: Audio-visual question answering on lightweight user devices requires balancing speed, memory usage, privacy, and communication. One approach is to run audio-visual encoders on a server and a lightweight language decoder on the user device, keeping questions local while offloading media encoding. However, transferring and processing the full multimodal representation remains costly. We propose a training-free, query-agnostic token pruning framework that constructs a compact, reusable memory before transmission. We define video spatio-temporal blocks and aligned audio windows as players and estimate their importance through Monte Carlo Shapley valuation. We use an embedding coverage objective that combines query-independent utility with cross-modal redundancy calibration and rewards unimodal diversity through marginal coverage gains. We retain the original token embeddings of high-scoring players under a limited token budget and transfer the resulting memory once for reuse across subsequent local questions. Experiments on Video-MME, Daily-Omni, and Video-Holmes using video-SALMONN 2+ (3B) and video-SALMONN 2 Pro (4B) show that our method achieves the best or tied-best QA accuracy among the compared compression methods across all evaluated settings. On Daily-Omni with video-SALMONN 2+ (3B) at 80\% pruning, our method improves accuracy by 12.86--14.62 percentage points over these methods while achieving the lowest device-side LLM prefill and decoding latency, with up to $1.92\times$ decoding speedup at comparable embedding memory usage.

</details>


### [156] [Event-Based Person Detection for Maritime UAV Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23088)
*Zijie Ning,Patrick Vandewalle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究用事件相机进行海上人员落水检测：基于v2e将MOBDrone救援视频合成事件流，比较事件视觉Transformer RVT-T与曝光匹配的图像检测器YOLOv8n、RT-DETR-R18，并在Jetson Orin Nano上评估机载实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上搜救需要快速定位水中人员，无人机巡查时相机运动与低光长曝光会造成小目标运动模糊，降低检测性能。事件相机以异步方式记录亮度变化，可减轻长曝光带来的运动模糊，因此值得探索其用于海上人员检测的可行性。

Method: 利用v2e事件模拟器从公开MOBDrone救援视频生成合成事件数据，并沿用原有标注；以事件模型RVT-T（循环视觉Transformer）与在曝光匹配图像上训练的YOLOv8n、RT-DETR-R18进行对比；设置50–200 ms合成曝光时间分析曝光影响；并将检测器部署于Jetson Orin Nano评估机载处理精度、时延与能耗。

Result: 在来自留出录像的843个测试样本上，100 ms合成曝光下RVT-T取得15.63% person AP50:95，YOLOv8n为6.89%、RT-DETR-R18为9.44%；曝光从50 ms增至200 ms时帧式检测精度逐步下降，与事件模型的差距进一步扩大。Jetson Orin Nano上RVT-T保持精度，约需136 ms与2.02 J每样本，可支持无人机实时处理。

Conclusion: 合成事件数据可作为海上人员检测的有效替代输入，事件模型在长曝光/运动模糊条件下显著优于帧式检测器，且机载部署可满足实时性与能耗要求，说明事件相机用于海上搜救人员检测具有可行性。

Abstract: Maritime search and rescue requires locating people in the water quickly, motivating the use of UAVs to survey the search area. During flight, camera motion and the longer exposures used in low light can blur small targets and hinder person detection. Event cameras record brightness changes as they occur, reducing the motion blur associated with longer exposure times. We investigate their use for maritime person detection by generating synthetic events using the v2e event simulator from public MOBDrone rescue videos and retaining the existing annotations. We compare the recurrent vision transformer model RVT-T with YOLOv8n and RT-DETR-R18 trained on exposure-matched images. On 843 test samples from held-out recordings, RVT-T achieves 15.63% person AP50:95, compared with 6.89% for YOLOv8n and 9.44% for RT-DETR-R18 under 100 ms synthetic exposure. Increasing exposure times from 50 to 200 ms progressively reduces frame-based detection accuracy, widening the gap to the event model. To evaluate the feasibility of on-board processing, we deploy our detector on a Jetson Orin Nano platform. The RVT-T model retains its accuracy and requires approximately 136 ms and 2.02 J per sample, allowing real-time processing on the UAV.

</details>


### [157] [FMT: Lossless Image Compression in a Fixed Number of Parallel Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23108)
*Ammar Ali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FMT是一种无损图像格式，将解码顺序与熵编码器协同设计为粗到细的固定步数多尺度树，使任意尺寸图像均以常数个串行步骤解码；并在其上构建从无网络规则到大网络的位精确编解码器族，在大小与速度上均优于对比方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式无损编解码器压缩率优于经典编解码器，但解码耗时达秒级（经典为毫秒级），且加速变体会损失大部分压缩收益；作者认为主要瓶颈是"等待"而非"计算"：网络等待条件值、算术解码器逐符号串行读取。

Method: 设计FMT格式：图像表示为粗到细的多尺度树，各步骤持有独立值；GPU一次性预测整步并用以并行rANS lane解码，lane间状态逐步传递，从而解码时延不随图像尺寸增长。基于该树构建一族位精确（bit-exact）编解码器，涵盖无神经网络规则到大型网络。

Result: 在同一GPU上，无编解码器能在大小与速度两方面同时胜过FMT族任一成员；无网络版本在大小与速度上均优于NVIDIA HTJ2K；大网络版本在相同或更小体积下，解码Kodak图像比同A100上运行的学习式编解码器快9–61倍。

Conclusion: 通过协同设计解码顺序与熵编码，可将学习式无损编解码的串行等待转化为固定步数并行解码，兼顾压缩率与速度，使学习式编解码具备实用化潜力。

Abstract: Learned lossless codecs make smaller files than common classical codecs, yet the images we archive and train on still use classical codecs. A main reason is time: a learned codec takes seconds to decode a photo that a classical codec opens in milliseconds, and their faster variants give up most of their gain in size. We argue that much of this time is spent waiting, not computing: the network waits for the values it conditions on, and the arithmetic decoder reads them one at a time. We introduce FMT (Fixed-step Multiscale Tree), a lossless image format whose decoding order and entropy coder are designed together so that neither wait grows with the image. FMT stores an image as a coarse-to-fine tree whose steps hold independent values: a GPU predicts each step at once and decodes it in parallel rANS lanes that carry their states from step to step, so an image of any size decodes in a fixed number of sequential steps. On this tree we build a family of bit-exact codecs, from a rule with no network to large networks, and no codec we ran on the same GPU beats any of them on both size and speed. Without a network, FMT beats NVIDIA's HTJ2K on size and speed; with a large one, it decodes Kodak photos 9 to 61X faster than learned codecs we ran on the same A100, at the same size or smaller.

</details>


### [158] [Event-Based Dynamic Lip Region Estimation During Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23111)
*Hamze Hammami,Omar Eldardeer,Francesco Rea*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用连续时间循环网络（CT-RNN）在完整事件流上直接回归唇部稠密热力图，实现事件相机下的唇部空间定位，无需裁剪ROI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备高时间分辨率与低功耗，适合低照度、高动态范围场景下的唇读、视听语音分析与机器人交互；但现有事件唇读、说话人识别与语音活动检测多依赖手工或数据集给定的ROI，仅给出嘴部关键点，与应用所需的区域定位不匹配。

Method: 将事件编码为按极性分离的体素网格；在每个空间位置用双通路连续时间循环网络处理：快速无状态通路负责精化，慢速有状态通路负责锚定；时间常数在对数空间有界且作用于每个时间bin的真实流逝时间。另测试加入空间编码器。

Result: 在四位未见说话人上唇部命中率达69.5%，目标切换仅2.1次/秒，而参数更多的卷积基线为8.9与11.0；加入空间编码器提升精度但在新的人脸尺度上失效；在真实事件传感器上（仅用仿真事件训练）达58.0%，CNN为43.2%，且切换次数不到其一半。

Conclusion: 从全事件帧直接预测稠密唇部热力图是可行的，CT-RNN在精度与时间稳定性上均优于卷积基线，并具备仿真到真实的迁移能力。

Abstract: Event cameras offer high temporal resolution and low power consumption, making them promising for speech-related applications. These include automatic lip reading, audiovisual speech analysis, and human-robot interaction, particularly in challenging environments characterized by low illumination or high dynamic range. Event-based lip reading, speaker recognition, and voice activity detection, however, mostly operate on a cropped region of interest (ROI), by hand or by the dataset, and no event-based method supplies that ROI as a spatial region; existing event-based face analysis gives the mouth as landmarks, which presents a mismatch between application and localization. The present work predicts a dense heatmap over the lip region from the full event "frame". Events are encoded as a polarity-separated voxel grid and processed at every location by a two-pathway continuous-time recurrent network (CT-RNN) with learned, log-space bounded time constants acting on the real elapsed time of each bin: a stateless fast pathway and a stateful slow pathway, utilised for refining and anchoring the prediction. The model reaches 69.5% on lips on four unseen speakers and switches target only 2.1 times per second, against 8.9 and 11.0 for convolutional baselines with
  more parameters. Adding a spatial encoder improves precision but fails on a new face scale. On a real event sensor, trained only on simulated events, the proposed model reaches 58.0% on lips against 43.2% for the CNN, with fewer than half its target switches.

</details>


### [159] [LUTBench: Benchmarking Text-Driven Photographic Look Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23113)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Boyu Wei,Chengkun Yue,Xuanhua Yin,Yiyang Li,Chupeng Liu,Zihan Deng,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LUTBench：基于 91 个 3D LUT 与 MIT-Adobe FiveK 构建 455,000 对可复现的输入-目标图像对，并配套模板化、可验证的文本描述生成流程，为文本驱动的照片调色迁移提供统一评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动调色研究在数据集、文本指令、目标定义和评价指标上各不相同，导致不同方法复现同一“look”的保真度难以横向比较，缺乏共享协议下的公平基准。

Method: 收集 91 个三维查找表（3D LUT），为每个 LUT 定义一种照片风格并指定输入转换；将风格应用于 5,000 张 MIT-Adobe FiveK 照片，生成 455,000 对输入-目标图像对。文本侧：在固定色样上测量色调与色彩变化，经模板转化为候选子句，再用训练图像测量验证子句可用性后组装成描述。评测包含目标保真度、对预定义输入转换的敏感性，以及将“未见风格泛化”与“已见风格在新照片上的复现”分离考察。

Result: 在 16 种方法上的对比显示，更强的文本对齐并不必然带来更高的目标保真度；但多数有监督变体在已见与未见风格上均提升了保真度。

Conclusion: 文本对齐与目标保真度并非一致优化目标，仅以文本相似度衡量调色迁移会产生误导；统一协议下的评测能更清晰地刻画方法的泛化能力，有监督训练是提升保真度的有效途径。

Abstract: Photographic color grading shapes the tone and color of a photograph to achieve a desired look. Text-driven photographic look transfer lets users specify these adjustments in natural language, with the goal of reproducing the requested look across photographs while preserving their content. However, prior research on text-driven color grading uses different datasets, text instructions, target definitions, and metrics. These differences make it difficult to compare how faithfully the methods reproduce the same look. To compare methods under a shared protocol, we introduce LUTBench. We collect 91 three-dimensional lookup tables (3D LUTs) and define one photographic look per LUT with a specified input conversion. Applying these looks to 5,000 MIT-Adobe FiveK photographs produces 455,000 input-target image pairs with reproducible target images. For each look, we use templates to turn measured tone and color changes on fixed color samples into candidate clauses. We use training image measurements to verify applicable clauses before composing the text description. The evaluation measures target fidelity, tests sensitivity to predefined input conversions, and separates generalization to unseen looks from reproduction of seen looks on new photographs. Across 16 methods, stronger text alignment does not guarantee target fidelity. However, most supervised variants improve fidelity on both seen and unseen looks.

</details>


### [160] [Nunchi: Ears Help Eyes in Multi-Party Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23115)
*JunYeong Park,Shijing Wang,Seonwook Park,Michael Stengel,Shalini De Mello,Sang-Il Choi,Hyung Jin Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 SORBET 注视基准与 Nunchi 框架，融合注视几何与对话线索（语音、转录文本）来识别多人在对话中被注视者，比最强基线高 4.8 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人在场时仅凭注视几何难以消歧被注视对象；对话中的说话人身份与话语内容是几何信息缺失的重要线索，但现有注视数据集缺乏同步语音、转录与多人场景标注。

Method: 构建 SORBET 基准：1,578 段视频、3,340 个样本，含同步语音、转录与被注视者标签，多人场景覆盖度超过现有数据集。提出 Nunchi：由转录、注视模型与说话人模型输出构造文本提示，将各类线索按共享标签关联到候选人物，再由视觉语言模型（VLM）权衡注视几何与对话线索进行判定。

Result: Nunchi 较最强现有方法提升 4.8 个百分点；在 SORBET 上对话线索有效而额外视觉线索无效；增益在三个 VLM、自动转录以及两个现有数据集上均成立。

Conclusion: 对话线索是多人注视目标识别的关键补充，VLM 框架可有效整合异构线索并具跨模型、跨数据集泛化性。

Abstract: Identifying whom a person looks at is central to understanding multi-party conversations, yet gaze geometry alone becomes ambiguous as more people appear. Conversations offer cues that geometry lacks, such as who is speaking and what is said, but no gaze dataset provides them for many multi-party scenes. We introduce SORBET, a benchmark of 3,340 samples from 1,578 clips with synchronized speech, transcripts, and attended person labels, covering more multi-party scenes than existing gaze datasets. Prior gaze models struggle to use these cues, which come in different forms, must be linked to the right person, and can conflict with gaze geometry. We propose Nunchi, a framework that constructs a text prompt from the transcript and the outputs of gaze and speaker models, links every cue to the candidates with shared labels, and lets a vision-language model (VLM) weigh gaze geometry against conversational cues to identify the attended person. Nunchi outperforms the strongest existing method by 4.8 percentage points. On SORBET, conversational cues help whereas additional visual cues do not, and the gains hold across three VLMs, with automatic transcripts, and on two existing datasets.

</details>


### [161] [GameRealm: Grounding Visual Learning in Interactive Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23140)
*Weiguang Zhao,Yifan Wang,Ni Yang,Yating Wang,Xingyu Miao,Yankai Jiang,Mulin Yu,Tao Lu,Haoyu Guo,Junting Dong,Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出大规模游戏原生标注数据集 GameRealm（超1.2亿RGB帧、1000小时游戏画面），提供度量深度、相机参数、同步音频、控制输入与3D点轨迹等多模态监督，并配套统一采集流水线，实验证明其可提升视觉生成与几何学习类基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型是物理智能的核心，但受限于训练数据碎片化、标注质量不一致、多模态监督不足。

Method: 构建 GameRealm 数据集：覆盖单视图、立体、多视图、全景等多种视角配置，帧与游戏原生信号同步以获得精确标注；设计结合人工操作与智能体探索的统一数据采集流水线，实现跨游戏环境的可扩展获取。

Result: 在多种视觉学习任务上的大量实验表明，GameRealm 在视觉生成与几何学习两方面均一致地增强视觉基础模型。

Conclusion: GameRealm 是通用且有价值的数据资源，为推进视觉智能提供新机遇。

Abstract: Visual foundation models are central to physical intelligence, yet their development remains constrained by fragmented training datasets, inconsistent annotation quality, and limited multimodal supervision. To address these challenges, we introduce GameRealm, a large-scale, richly annotated dataset that grounds visual observations in synchronized, game-native supervision. GameRealm encompasses over 120 million RGB frames and 1,000 hours of gameplay footage across all views. Beyond its unprecedented scale, GameRealm provides rich multimodal supervision, including metric depth, camera parameters, synchronized audio, control inputs, and 3D point trajectories. Its diversity further extends to flexible view configurations, spanning single-view, stereo, multi-view, and panoramic recordings. To enable scalable acquisition across diverse game environments, we develop a unified data collection pipeline that combines human-operated interaction with agentic exploration. Extensive experiments across diverse visual learning tasks demonstrate that GameRealm consistently strengthens visual foundation models in both visual generation and geometric learning. These results establish GameRealm as a versatile and valuable data resource, unlocking new opportunities for advancing visual intelligence.

</details>


### [162] [$π^4$: A Foundation Model for 4D Scene Geometry and Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23144)
*Yifan Wang,Weiguang Zhao,Ni Yang,Haoyu Guo,Junting Dong,Weinan Zhang,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一4D场景感知基础模型π⁴，从无标定RGB输入联合恢复几何、相机位姿、度量尺度、2D/3D点轨迹与运动物体掩码，约0.5B参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D场景感知任务（几何重建、相机估计、点跟踪、运动分割）通常由独立模型分别处理，缺乏统一框架；且多数方法依赖标定输入、参数量大，难以在单图、无序图像集与视频等多样化野外输入上通用。

Method: 将几何分支与受几何条件约束的运动分支耦合：几何分支从无标定观测重建稠密几何、相机位姿与度量尺度；运动分支预测图像空间对应关系，并利用重建的目标视图几何将其提升至3D。两分支共享骨干网络，一次前向即输出重建与轨迹；采用数据与训练配方，使最大64视图训练上下文可泛化至上千视图的大规模场景。

Result: 在多类基准上取得几何评测最优综合平均排名；单视图与多视图2D/3D点跟踪及运动物体分割具竞争力；全系统约0.5B参数，不足多数基线一半；单次前向输出联合重建与跟踪，并支持数千输入图像的大场景。

Conclusion: π⁴以紧凑的统一架构实现从无标定RGB到4D场景感知的端到端建模，兼具精度、效率与可扩展性；论文同时公开数据与训练配方，并给出关键设计选择的实证分析。

Abstract: $π^4$ is a unified foundation model for 4D scene perception. From diverse, uncalibrated RGB observations in the wild---a single image, an unordered collection, or a video sequence---it recovers dense geometry, camera poses, and metric scale, together with 2D/3D point tracks and moving-object masks for multi-image inputs. It couples a geometry branch with a geometry-conditioned motion branch, which predicts image-space correspondences that are lifted into 3D using reconstructed target-view geometry. Across diverse benchmarks, $π^4$ achieves the best overall average rank in geometry evaluation, alongside competitive performance in single- and multiview 2D/3D point tracking and moving-object segmentation. With a shared backbone, the full geometry-and-motion system contains approximately 0.5B parameters---less than half the size of most baselines. It produces joint reconstructions and tracks in one forward pass, including large-scale scenes with thousands of input images despite training with contexts of at most 64 views. We further present the data and training recipe underlying these capabilities, together with empirical studies of key design choices. Code and models are available at https://github.com/yyfz/Pi4.

</details>


### [163] [VIVID: Beyond Static Multi-Image Reasoning with Localized Visual Differences and Textual Entity Dynamics in Video MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23153)
*Chengqian Ma,Yuanze Zeng,Hongyu Wang,Mingfeng Lin,YiCheng Xiao,Yu Chen,Pengyu Li,WenHao Wang,Yuexiao Ma,Xiawu Zheng,Fei Chao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VIVID，通过帧间Patch Diff与增量驱动关键帧规划，在极少可训练参数下提升紧预算视频问答的局部运动与事件顺序理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧帧预算下现有视频MLLM把理解退化为静态多图推理：空间上整帧池化使小运动目标被背景稀释，时间上丢弃中间帧或平均token抹除帧间运动，导致方向、速度与顺序判断混乱。

Method: 模仿人类视觉，关键帧捕获核心状态、帧间Patch Diff保留相邻变化（限定于K_f个关键帧间）。ADKP从3fps帧池中选取锚点并将周边patch差异送入双路变化记忆DRCM，以零额外锚点token形成Dynamic Tokens；配合相机稳定的主动运动ROI与按时间排序的实体状态链。

Result: 在冻结27B骨干上仅增加0.22%可训练参数；VIVID-Small（50个样本、约1.5 GPU小时）在8,083个问题上总体准确率超过全部11个基线，仅落后VIVID-Large（3,600样本），后者在MotionBench、Video-MME、TempCompass上领先。

Conclusion: 以少量可训练参数注入帧间动态信息，可在紧帧预算下显著提升视频MLLM的运动与事件顺序理解，并具备良好数据效率。

Abstract: Video question answering under tight frame budgets requires preserving localized motion and event order, yet current video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) reduce comprehension to static multi-image reasoning by discarding unselected frames and encoding keyframes independently: (1) Spatially, whole-frame pooling dilutes small moving targets into static backgrounds; (2) Temporally, dropping intermediate frames or averaging tokens erases inter-frame motion, confusing direction, speed, and order. We propose VIVID (Visual Inter-frame Variations and Individual Dynamics), which moves beyond static snapshots like human vision: keyframes capture core states while inter-frame Patch Diffs preserve neighboring changes within $K_f$ keyframes. Autonomous Delta-Driven Keyframe Planning (ADKP) selects anchors from a $3\text{ fps}$ pool and feeds surrounding patch differences into Dual-Road Change Memory (DRCM), forming Dynamic Tokens at zero extra anchor tokens alongside a camera-stabilized Active Motion Region of Interest (ROI) and chronological Entity State Chains. Adding $0.22\%$ trainable parameters to a frozen $27\text{B}$ backbone, VIVID-Small ($50$ examples, $\sim 1.5$ GPU hours) beats all eleven baselines in overall accuracy across $8,083$ questions and trails only VIVID-Large ($3,600$ examples), which leads on MotionBench, Video-MME, and TempCompass.

</details>


### [164] [TextSphere: Efficient Text-to-Image Generation via Shared Hyperspherical Alignment and Uniformity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23155)
*Yixia Zhang,Girish A. Koushik,Xilu Wang,Wenwu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TextSphere，将Sphere Encoder扩展为文本条件生成框架，用AdaLN注入CLIP文本嵌入并设计SSAU目标实现球面语义对齐，在少步数下取得优于扩散/GAN基线的gFID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sphere Encoder推理步数少、效率高，但其类条件设计缺乏跨模态对齐能力，难以直接扩展到自由文本条件生成。

Method: 通过AdaLN将预训练CLIP文本嵌入注入生成过程，保留原有球面采样机制；提出Spherical Semantic Alignment and Uniformity (SSAU)目标，在超球面上联合优化语义对齐与表示均匀性；并用PLS-SVD分析跨模态对齐与模态间隙。

Result: 在CUB-200-2011、MS-COCO-2014、Flickr30k上，gFID较文本条件基线一致下降，rFID低于1.0；仅用4步（CUB）和6步（MS-COCO）采样即低于从头训练的扩散与GAN模型gFID；并发现重建保真度提升未必带来更好生成质量。

Conclusion: TextSphere以极少采样步数实现高效文本到图像生成，SSAU有效弥合跨模态表示鸿沟；重建保真度与生成质量并非正相关，PLS-SVD为分析跨模态对齐提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image generation have substantially improved image quality, particularly through diffusion-based methods that require multiple inference steps. In contrast, Sphere Encoder offers an efficient generative framework capable of producing images with fewer forward steps. However, extending it to free-form text is nontrivial, because its class-conditioned design lacks cross-modal alignment. To address this challenge, we propose TextSphere, an efficient text-to-image generative framework that extends Sphere Encoder with language conditioning and cross-modal alignment. We incorporate pretrained CLIP text embeddings through adaptive layer normalization (AdaLN) to guide image generation while retaining the original spherical sampling mechanism. To bridge the cross-modal representation gap, we introduce a Spherical Semantic Alignment and Uniformity (SSAU) objective that jointly promotes semantic alignment and representation uniformity on the hypersphere. Experiments on CUB-200-2011, MS-COCO-2014, and Flickr30k demonstrate that TextSphere consistently reduces generation FID (gFID) over the text-conditioned baseline while achieving reconstruction FID (rFID) below 1.0. With only 4 and 6 sampling steps, TextSphere achieves lower gFID than the compared diffusion- and GAN-based models trained from scratch on CUB-200-2011 and MS-COCO-2014, respectively. We further observe that improved reconstruction fidelity does not necessarily yield better generation quality. In addition, we use Partial Least Squares Singular Value Decomposition (PLS-SVD) to analyze cross-modal alignment and modality gap, offering insight into the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [165] [Attribution Through the Decoder: Prediction-Space Explanations for Structured Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23161)
*Zaer Bin Zaqy,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 预测角度、视线方向与旋转的网络不直接输出这些量，而是输出原始坐标再经固定解码器映射。常规归因施加在原始坐标上，会把解码器丢弃的变化（如向量的模长）也归给输入。本文提出通过解码器微分做归因，对归一化解码器即化为缩放的切向投影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归因方法通常作用于网络输出的原始坐标，但真正决定预测的是固定解码器（如归一化）之后的量。因此归因可能把"只改变模长、不改变方向"这类对预测无影响的输入变化计入相关性，产生虚假重要性和误导性解释。作者希望区分两类失败来源：径向污染（radial contamination）与标量化损失（scalarization loss）。

Method: 提出"通过解码器微分"的归因框架：对归一化解码器，梯度化为按比例缩放的切向投影（tangent projection）；形式化刻画对输出重缩放的不变性，证明球面上任意固定光滑标量目标都存在切向敏感度为零的点；比较了端点投影版 Integrated Gradients（仍可把相关性归给纯尺度输入）与直接角度版 Integrated Gradients（不能）；并在 Grad-CAM、LayerCAM 上构造切向变体（含双极性切向 LayerCAM），部分变体需修改归因聚合方式。

Result: 合成任务（相关性解析已知）：环境坐标归因最高把 72% 的相关性质量分配到无关区域。MPIIGaze 验证集：10% 像素删除下切向 Grad-CAM 的预测位移比环境归因高 44%。PASCAL3D+（6D 旋转解码器）：完整微分比环境坐标求和平均删除位移改善 14.1°，比解码器不变标量改善 13.8°。双极性切向 LayerCAM 结果混合：在循环回归上优于环境求和 LayerCAM，在四元数姿态上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 结果将失效来源分为径向污染与标量化损失两类。方法本身复用现有归因方法，只需换成解码器感知的目标，部分变体还需调整聚合方式。但不变的归因仍可能漏掉真正驱动预测的输入，因此不变性与删除性能应分别检验，不能只凭不变性判定解释质量。

Abstract: Networks that predict angles, gaze directions, and rotations do not output these quantities directly: they emit raw coordinates that a fixed decoder, such as normalization, maps to the final prediction. Attribution methods are usually applied to the raw coordinates, so they can credit inputs for changes the decoder discards, such as rescaling a vector when only its direction matters. The resulting explanation can highlight inputs that have no effect on the prediction. We instead attribute through the decoder differential, which for normalization decoders reduces to a scaled tangent projection. We characterize invariance to output rescaling, show that every fixed smooth scalar target on a sphere has points of zero tangential sensitivity, and show that an endpoint-projected variant of Integrated Gradients can still attribute to scale-only inputs, whereas direct angular Integrated Gradients cannot. On synthetic tasks with analytically known relevance, ambient attribution assigns up to 72% of its mass to an irrelevant region. On MPIIGaze validation data, tangent Grad-CAM produces 44% greater prediction displacement under 10% pixel deletion. On PASCAL3D+ with a 6D rotation decoder, the full differential improves mean deletion displacement by 14.1 degrees over the ambient coordinate sum and by 13.8 degrees over a decoder-invariant scalar. Results for a two-polarity tangent LayerCAM variant are mixed: it exceeds ambient-sum LayerCAM on circular regression but no difference is detected on quaternion pose. These results separate two failure sources, radial contamination and scalarization loss. The approach uses existing attribution methods with decoder-aware targets, although some variants also require changes to attribution aggregation. An invariant attribution can still miss inputs that drive the prediction, so invariance and deletion performance should each be checked.

</details>


### [166] [Déjà View: A Shared Working-Memory Model for Visual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23177)
*Mranmay Shetty,Gabriel Kreiman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个融合动态活动与图像特征间临时关联的工作记忆模型，用同一套训练参数在五个任务上灵活切换，并考察延迟、干扰与图像历史对记忆表现的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作记忆系统必须既能保持刺激，又能灵活地调用记忆完成不同任务；作者希望用单一模型解释这种灵活性，并系统研究延迟、分心刺激与先前图像经历如何影响记忆错误。

Method: 构建面向图像的工作记忆模型，将动态神经活动与图像特征之间的临时关联相结合；在五个任务上联合训练（含"呈现其他图像时记住样本""记忆样本的同时对干扰图像分类""判断图像是否为新/旧"），由显式任务线索引导同一模型产生相应反应，并操纵延迟、干扰数量与目标图像出现的历史远近。

Result: 在所测延迟范围内，延迟对样本记忆表现影响很小；看到更多图像会导致更多虚假报警，即使目标图像在各条件下出现的新近程度相同；在"样本记忆+分类"任务中，随着提问数量增加，所有问题的正确率都下降，尽管样本记忆本身保持稳健；模型在每种任务40个短试次中表现稳定。

Conclusion: 单一工作记忆系统可被灵活用于多种任务，其错误同时依赖于先前图像经历与任务需求，而非仅由记忆痕迹衰减决定。

Abstract: A working-memory model must remember stimuli and use its memory flexibly to perform different tasks. Here we introduce a model of working memory for images that combines dynamic activity with temporary associations between image features. We train the model on five tasks, including remembering a sample while other images are presented, categorizing distractor images while remembering the sample, and determining whether an image is new or has been presented before. The same trained model performs all tasks, guided by explicit cues about which response is required. We use this flexible memory model to ask how delay, distraction, and image history affect performance. Delays have little effect on sample-memory performance for the delays we tested, while seeing more images leads to more false alarms even when the target image appeared equally recently in all cases. On the sample-memory-plus-categorization task, the probability of correct responses on all questions decreases as more questions are asked, despite robust sample-memory performance. Finally, the model maintains stable performance across 40 brief trials of each task under the tested conditions. Together, these results demonstrate how a single working-memory system can be flexibly used to perform different tasks, and how its errors depend on both previous images and task demands.

</details>


### [167] [Say Less, Hallucinate Less? Diagnosing and Restoring Object Coverage in Preference-Optimized Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23179)
*Hansang Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现偏好优化（PO）在降低大视觉语言模型物体幻觉的同时会降低物体覆盖度，且其部分增益可被基座模型的早停复现；据此提出无需训练的"基座引导物体类别恢复"方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化被视为减少大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）物体幻觉的标准手段，但作者发现四个公开的LLaVA-1.5-7B PO检查点在降低幻觉率的同时也降低了物体覆盖度（命名类别更少），在CHAIR的集合级物体F1上并无显著收益。因此需要重新审视PO增益的来源，并寻求不牺牲召回的方法。

Method: 1）用EOS logit偏置控制解码停止时机，考察基座模型通过早停能复现多少PO增益；2）分析负EOS偏置（延长生成）能否补回缺失类别；3）提出base-guided object-category restoration：将高置信度的"仅基座命名"类别追加到PO字幕中，无需训练，阈值在开发集上确定；4）采用召回受控的评估方式，与召回匹配的基座早停曲线对比。

Result: 正EOS偏置使基座模型达到各PO模型的召回，仅早停即可在两组不相交COCO集上复现三个检查点58%–86%的CHAIRi增益；更长生成无法带回丢失类别；贪心解码下负EOS偏置仅恢复2%–9%的丢失召回，且94%–96%的新增物体提及是重复已命名类别。在CHAIR上，同一图像的基座字幕仍命名约一半被遗漏的类别。所提方法在留出COCO集上使三个检查点召回与基座差距在1个百分点内，且独特物体幻觉率低于基座模型；相对召回匹配的基座早停曲线，其增益在RLAIF-V接近基座召回时成立，在中间召回水平下RLAIF-V与CHAIR-DPO亦成立。

Conclusion: PO的幻觉降低在很大程度上可由基座模型的早停行为解释，而PO实际损害了物体覆盖度；基座模型保留了PO遗漏类别的信息。所提出的基座引导类别恢复方法可在无训练条件下恢复召回、同时保持较低幻觉率，并强调需要召回受控的评估协议。

Abstract: Preference optimization (PO) is the standard way to reduce object hallucination in large vision-language models. We find that four public PO checkpoints of LLaVA-1.5-7B also lower object coverage. They name fewer object categories, and their lower hallucination rates bring no significant gain in set-level object F1 on CHAIR. We therefore ask how much of the PO gain the base model can reproduce by stopping earlier. A positive end-of-sequence (EOS) logit bias lets the base model reach the recall of each PO model, and this early stopping alone reproduces 58 to 86 percent of the CHAIRi gain of three checkpoints on two disjoint COCO sets. Writing longer does not bring the lost categories back. Under greedy decoding, a negative EOS bias recovers only 2 to 9 percent of the lost CHAIR recall of these checkpoints, and 94 to 96 percent of the added object mentions repeat categories already named. On CHAIR, the base caption of the same image still names about half of the missed object categories. We therefore propose base-guided object-category restoration, which appends high-confidence base-only categories to the PO caption without training. With thresholds fixed on a development set, it brings three checkpoints within one point of the base recall on a held-out COCO set with a lower distinct-object hallucination rate than the base model. Against the recall-matched base stopping curve, its gain holds for RLAIF-V near the base recall and for RLAIF-V and CHAIR-DPO at an intermediate recall. We will release the code of the recall-controlled evaluation.

</details>


### [168] [Any2Building: Unifying Point Clouds and Images for Building Reconstruction via Shared Spatial Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23187)
*Hongye Hou,Zhuoxiao Li,Liu Xiangyu,Tengxi Wang,Jing Ou,Dongli Wu,Shuai Zhang,Tongyan Hua,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Any2Building统一模型，可从部分点云、俯视图像或两者重建LoD2建筑；核心为占用锚定的共享空间查询表示，并用体素条件表面生成与低模重网格化得到紧凑模型，同时发布BuildingWorld-Image数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一建筑的部分点云与俯视图像分别提供不完整的三维表面测量与关于占地轮廓、屋顶几何和屋顶附属结构的视觉线索；二者互补，因此需要一个在三维中对齐几何与视觉线索、并在单模态或双模态输入下均能重建建筑的统一框架。

Method: Any2Building支持输入部分点云、俯视图像或二者组合，并可选用粗几何先验。其核心是占用锚定的共享空间查询表示：将可用观测组织到公共三维网格中，以完整建筑表面体素为监督，使查询把观测证据与目标几何关联，令共享生成器跨任务学习结构先验，并为缺失结构恢复提供空间引导。随后训练好的体素条件表面生成与低模重网格化流程把预测结构转为紧凑LoD2模型。配套数据集BuildingWorld-Image含约一百万配对样本，覆盖18座城市。

Result: 在多基准实验中，Any2Building在单模态与多模态点云补全、单图建筑生成及先验辅助生成任务上，几何重建精度均优于现有方法；当点云输入模态退化时仍能保持细节丰富且稳健的恢复。

Conclusion: 在三维中统一对齐几何与视觉线索的共享表示，可有效统一多模态建筑重建任务，并借助跨任务结构先验实现缺失结构恢复与鲁棒LoD2建模。

Abstract: Abstracting structured buildings from limited observations is essential for 3D urban modeling and digital twins. Partial point clouds and overhead images of the same building provide incomplete 3D surface measurements and visual cues to its footprint, roof geometry, and rooftop structures, respectively. This motivates a unified framework that aligns geometric and visual cues in 3D and reconstructs buildings from either or both modalities. Building on this connection, we present Any2Building, a unified model for Level of Detail 2 (LoD2) building reconstruction from partial point clouds, overhead images, or both, with optional coarse geometric priors. At its core is an occupancy-grounded shared spatial queries representation that organizes available observations within a common 3D grid. Supervised by complete building surface voxels, these queries connect observational evidence to target geometry, enabling a shared generator to learn structural priors across tasks and providing spatial guidance for recovering missing structure. A trained voxel conditional surface-generation and low-poly remeshing pipeline then converts the predicted structure into compact LoD2 building models. To support this framework, we introduce BuildingWorld-Image, comprising approximately one million paired samples of partial point clouds, overhead images, and LoD2 building models across 18 cities. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that Any2Building outperforms existing methods in geometric reconstruction accuracy across unimodal and multimodal point-cloud completion, single-image building generation, and prior-assisted generation, while maintaining detailed and robust recovery when point cloud input modality is degraded.

</details>


### [169] [Retrieval-Augmented Correction for Generalist Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23189)
*Vincenzo Marcianò,Xiaoming Zhang,Michela Antonelli,Sebastien Ourselin,Maria A Zuluaga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRACE：基于检索增强的通用分割掩膜修正器，单一模型横跨CT/MRI/超声多解剖结构，弱分割器输入DSC 0.386可提升至+0.35，无需按解剖逐一训练修正模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化分割后的残余结构误差目前需按解剖结构逐个训练修正器，导致修正模型数量随分割目标数量线性增长，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出GRACE检索增强修正器：在多器官语料（紧凑器官、腔体、分支血管、肿瘤病灶）上仅训练一次；在已验证先验病例构成的共享知识库上下文中编辑输入掩膜；在冻结的多模态编码器上加轻量形状感知投影头，将嵌入相似度映射为掩膜形状一致性，使知识库检索到的病例可作为形状先验。评估包括留出数据集迁移（固定知识库仅由训练数据构建）与留一解剖（新器官已验证病例无参数加入索引、修正器冻结），覆盖18个公开数据集、9种精修配置、7个弱/中/强上游分割器。

Result: 弱级输入DSC 0.386，GRACE最多提升+0.35 DSC；强分割器输入DSC 0.932时平均变化很小；对训练与索引中均未出现的成像模态，迁移效果部分有效且依解剖而异。

Conclusion: 检索条件化修正是解剖特异性修正模型的可行替代方案；跨未见模态迁移仍是开放研究方向。

Abstract: Automated segmentation is routinely used as the first step in quantitative image-based clinical workflows, where predicted masks are subsequently reviewed and corrected by human operators. Automated correction of the residual structural errors is currently addressed one anatomy at a time, so the number of trained correctors scales with the number of segmentation targets. We present GRACE, a retrieval-augmented refiner that edits an input mask under context drawn from a shared knowledge base of verified prior cases. The refiner is trained once on a multi-organ corpus spanning compact organs, cavities, branching vessels, and tumour lesions. In the held-out dataset-transfer evaluation, three held-out datasets query a fixed knowledge base built from training data only. A separate leave-one-anatomy-out experiment tests parameter-free index extension: verified cases of an unseen solid organ enter the knowledge base, and the refiner stays frozen. A light shape-aware projection head on a frozen multimodal encoder maps embedding similarity to the shape agreement of the masks, so the knowledge base returns cases that the refiner can use as a shape prior. We evaluate GRACE on 18 public datasets spanning CT, MRI and ultrasound, using nine refinement configurations and seven upstream segmentors that span weak, mid, and strong operating regimes. We report each change with the DSC of its input mask. On the weak tier, the input DSC is 0.386 and GRACE adds up to +0.35 DSC. On a strong segmentor, the input DSC is 0.932 and the mean change is small. Transfer to an imaging modality that is absent from training and from the index is partial and depends on the anatomy, and remains an open research direction. Our results suggest retrieval-conditioned refinement is a practical alternative to anatomy-specific correction models. Code: https://github.com/robustml-eurecom/grace

</details>


### [170] [Reading Scene States: Uncovering Safety Failures in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.23206)
